For source-index headwords, fix sampled all-caps residue such as `DEGENERACY OF MANKIND` to a quieter source form, but treat broad volume-6 all-caps parenthetical scans as candidate pools unless the Chinese headword is unclear or the source form dominates the reader surface; record the candidate count and confirmed repairs. Continue fresh-seed sampling until a whole round is clean, because repeated boundary persistence means the current run still has 0 consecutive PASS rounds.
- Round 057 bibliography, verse-lineation, foreign-quote, and index-boundary addendum: When a fresh seed samples bibliography rows such as `ORTON, Job`, `PILKINGTON, James`, `THACKERAY, W. M.`, `LANDOR, Walter Savage`, `O'LEARY, Rev. Arthur`, or `BOWEN, Emanuel` as P2 source-interface residue, repair confirmed rows into a Chinese reader-facing citation: Chinese name/title first, with the source name/title preserved in parentheses where useful. If the appendix still has broad raw-bibliography candidates, record the candidate pool separately from confirmed defects. Treat poems collapsed into one prose paragraph, retained English/Latin quotations without same-unit Chinese meaning, and volume-index lines long enough to be truncated by sampling as current-run audit triggers. Repair poem lineation with Markdown hard breaks, add immediate Chinese glosses for short retained source quotations, and split only index/reference lines into shorter complete reader-facing units; do not split ordinary prose merely because it is long. Rebuild and open a fresh seed because this round is `FAIL_REPAIRED`.
- Round 058 cross-file setup, stale-sample, and encoding-marker addendum: If a setup paragraph is the last paragraph of a file and the payload begins in the next unit, repair by summarizing the next unit's payload in the setup paragraph rather than making the reader wait across files. For short quoted payloads, join the quote into the setup unit; for long poems or letters, add a concise content bridge and keep the full source payload nearby. If a sampled bad string no longer exists in current `chapters/final`, `chapters/translated`, or generated XHTML, record it as stale sample text with exact zero-hit scans rather than editing unrelated text. Treat visible source-name mojibake such as `DAILL脡` as P1/P2 and repair to a source-supported form such as `Daillé`; broad mojibake-marker scans are candidate pools and require context before bulk editing. Rebuild and use a fresh seed because this round is `FAIL_REPAIRED`.
- Round 059 reader-unit aggregation, bibliography-interface, and foreign-quote addendum: If repeated random samples expose setup paragraphs ending in `：` or `：——`, quoted-letter paragraphs that are only one part of a continuous letter, or overlong volume-index paragraphs, first check whether the sampler is using true reader-facing audit units rather than isolated Markdown paragraphs. Repair the extraction rule so short setup+payload, continuous quoted text, and long index strings become intelligible review units; do not hide real defects by sampler changes alone. Separately scan confirmed text defects: raw bibliography rows that start with all-caps source-name order, title-only English bibliography rows, retained Latin/Italian/French snippets without same-unit Chinese meaning, table rows where English account labels dominate, poem lines flattened into prose, and broken metaphor literalizations. Fix confirmed hits with a Chinese reader interface while preserving source forms in parentheses where useful, add immediate target-language glosses for retained foreign quotes, remove table source-label residue, and restore poetic lineation. Rebuild and open a fresh seed because this round is `FAIL_REPAIRED`.
- Round 060 quoted-block structure addendum: When a sampler or review unit normalizes multi-paragraph letters, diary extracts, document headings, or short verse into a single run-on line, it can create false quote-boundary blockers and can also reveal real layout defects. Audit both layers. The reader-unit extractor should keep Markdown line breaks in sample text and merge consecutive paragraph blocks until Chinese quotation marks balance, so an addressee, date, salutation, signature, or document title is not reviewed alone. Then still repair confirmed target-text issues such as English month-order calques (`第十月`, `第一月`) or verse lines flattened after a colon. Rebuild, smoke-test the sampler with the failed seed, remove temporary sample output, and open a fresh seed because the repaired round is not a clean PASS.
- Round 061 foreign-gloss, dangling setup, and inscription-layout addendum: When a random sample exposes a retained Latin/French/English quote, source-title form, variant phrase, or monument/verse block, verify that the same reader-facing audit unit contains the Chinese meaning and enough payload to stand alone. Scan exact failed strings first (`Dum memor`, `quel sot ose`, `in company`, title variants, paragraphs ending in `如下版本：` or cross-unit wording such as `下一单元开头即引`), then inspect small contexts against the source before changing. Repair confirmed hits by adding immediate Chinese glosses, turning missing quote payloads into self-contained note prose, normalizing reader-facing title forms in the active glossary, and restoring long inscriptions into readable paragraphs with punctuation instead of flattened source-layout residue. If the issue is caused by sampling an English quote apart from its following `其意为` paragraph, repair the reader-unit extractor as well as the text surface. Rebuild, smoke-test the failed seed, and treat the repaired round as `FAIL_REPAIRED`; only a later fresh seed can count toward PASS.
- Round 062 verse-lineation and bibliography-interface addendum: If a sampled poem or translated verse is flattened into a paragraph with inline double spaces, scan the full book for non-table inline double-space poetry candidates, then separate confirmed poems into Markdown-visible lines while recording tables, errata, account rows, and foreign-quote-plus-gloss lines as exceptions. For setup sentences that promise a following answer, letter, or verse but end before the payload, either merge the payload into the reader unit or rewrite the setup as a self-contained summary. For bibliography appendices, distinguish confirmed sampled entries that deserve Chinese-facing name/title translations from broad original-language bibliography rows; the latter should be explicitly labelled as original bibliographic records for version checking rather than left as `作者原名` prose. Rebuild and open a fresh seed because the repaired round is not a clean PASS.
- Round 063 foreign-verse lineation extension: Do not treat a retained source-language poem as closed merely because a Chinese gloss follows. If the source witness has verse line breaks, preserve a visible source-line shape in the same reader-facing unit before the gloss. Add scans for quoted Latin/Italian/French/English verse blocks that contain a gloss marker such as `意为` but still flatten multiple source lines into one run-on line; repair by restoring line breaks and keeping the Chinese meaning adjacent.
- Round 064 cross-file setup and long-epitaph extension: A paragraph at the end of a unit that points to the next unit with `原话如下`, `读者可自行判断`, or a quoted compliment ending in a colon is still a blocker even if the payload exists in the next file. Rewrite the current unit so it contains a concise summary of the promised payload, and leave the full quote in the next unit. For long Latin epitaphs or inscriptions, retaining the source layout is acceptable only when the same reader-facing unit also gives a compact Chinese summary or translation.
- Round 065 index/verse interface extension: Treat index units and short verse quotations as reader-facing prose, not harmless apparatus. Scan sampled strings plus exact same-family triggers such as `iii. iii.`, malformed proverb variants (`Quos Deus null`, `Quem Deus vult perdere`), abnormal spaces after Chinese punctuation in index entries, and poems flattened into one paragraph with inline spaces. Repair confirmed hits by normalizing citation markers for the target reader, restoring source-supported foreign quote forms or preserving source oddities with clear Chinese context, removing spacing noise from index prose, and preserving visible verse lineation. Record source-text oddities as reader-interface normalizations rather than silently reproducing confusing forms; rebuild and open a fresh seed because the repaired round is not a clean PASS.
- Round 066 letter/bibliography/place-name interface extension: If a sample exposes only a letter heading, letter closing, or setup paragraph (`致...先生`, `我永远是，我亲爱的先生，`, `我在这里给出一篇：`, `他说，他——`, or a colon introducing a poem/excerpt), make the current reader unit self-contained by adding a concise payload summary or by merging the closing formula with the signature. For retained Latin poems or epitaphs, add a target-language summary before the original as well as any following full gist, so a sampled source block is not foreign-only. For bibliography rows and index headwords, put the Chinese reader label first (`伏尔泰`, `普鲁士腓特烈二世`, `挑翻`) and keep the source headword in parentheses only as evidence. For source spellings that are historical variants of an established place (`Islam` for Ilam), keep one target name and explain the source spelling as `原文作 ...` rather than creating a second Chinese place name.
- Round 067 high-impact style, polysemy, and index-interface extension: If a fresh seed catches stiff preface prose, false-friend headwords, or long index units, treat them as book-wide candidate families, not isolated polish. Scan exact failed target phrases such as `这样一种状态`, `人类恩主`, `颇为少见的小书`, `领主的合理性`, `喂坏`, `滴温酒`, slash-flattened verse, and mechanical index fragments such as `缺少温和`, `做手势，反感`, `幽灵：见幽灵`, or `于某事多于另一事`. Repair confirmed hits by rewriting high-impact opening prose into natural target-language paragraphs, choosing context-bound legal/food/emotional terms (`酌定`, `喂养不良`, `慰心甘露`), preserving verse lineation with XHTML-safe poem blocks when Markdown hard breaks collapse, and splitting or smoothing only the sampled long index units whose Chinese interface is unclear. Merge dangling letter closings with signatures in the same reader unit. Rebuild, scan generated XHTML for the rejected strings, and open a fresh seed because the repaired round is not a clean PASS.
- Round 068 note/index boundary extension: If a random sample begins with an index continuation or ends with a source quote/setup before the payload, repair the reader-facing unit rather than treating it as harmless pagination. Scan exact failed fragments such as `由霍姆博士答复，同上`, `^关于约翰生的轶事`, `莫根，莫里斯，。`, `传种（PROPAGATE）`, and setup phrases like `下面两行诗` or retained source quotes such as `Nihil tetigit quod non ornavit`. Fix confirmed hits by restoring the missing headword in the current unit, moving cross-file continuation material back under its headword, replacing damaged punctuation-only entries with complete source-backed entries, choosing a non-crude target headword (`传播理解力` rather than biological `传种`), and adding an immediate Chinese meaning for retained Latin or other source quotes. Rebuild and rescan final Markdown plus generated XHTML for the rejected fragments before opening a fresh seed.
- Round 069 literary/interface addendum: If a fresh seed exposes opening-prose stiffness, dangling setup/payload splits, retained foreign verse without same-unit target meaning, long index continuations without headwords, or flattened poem/song lines, treat them as one reader-facing interface family. Scan exact failed target strings first, including setup endings such as `说道：`, `以下诗行——`, `鲍斯威尔注——`, foreign quote witnesses such as `Et variis albae`, title/OCR forms such as `Dietionarius`, index continuation starts such as `^v. 349` or `日期，忽视`, metaphor false literals such as `用小册子打我`, and verse slashes or inline song runs. Repair confirmed hits by moving short payloads into the same unit, adding immediate Chinese gist for retained Latin/foreign verse, restoring or prefixing the current index headword with `（续）`, splitting only overlong index/reference paragraphs at natural headword boundaries, rewriting high-impact opening prose into idiomatic target language, and preserving poem/song lineation with XHTML-safe poem blocks when needed. Record stale sample strings with exact zero-hit scans rather than editing unrelated current text; rebuilt rounds with these repairs remain non-PASS and require a fresh seed.
- Round 070 index-continuation and poem-block addendum: If a random sample begins inside a volume index entry, recover the governing headword from the previous paragraph or previous unit and prefix the sampled unit with `词头（SOURCE HEADWORD）（续）：`; do this for both file-start continuations and later paragraph continuations in the same file. Use exact sampled starts such as `因惧怕斯摩莱特`, `酿酒厂——利润`, `安排地产继承`, `弗格森是`, `士兵，像`, `只排除一种祸患`, `衣着朴素`, and `iii. 271；v. 150` as low-token scan triggers, then inspect only the nearby index flow before changing. In the same audit pass, treat one-line songs or verse quotations and raw bibliography rows as reader-interface defects: convert confirmed flattened poems to XHTML-safe poem blocks, change dangling `他说——` bridges to complete quote introductions, and normalize source-first rows such as `BRITISH MUSEUM MSS.` into target-language bibliography prose while preserving the source form in parentheses. Rebuild, scan final/translated/generated outputs for the rejected starts and old raw rows, and open a fresh seed because any repaired round is not a clean PASS.
- Round 071 bracket-note sampler, title-gloss, and raw-index addendum: If a random sample aggregates a quoted letter with bracket-numbered notes such as `[80]`, first fix the reader-unit extractor so bracket-number note paragraphs are `caption_note` units instead of being swallowed by quote-balancing. Still repair confirmed surface defects in the same round: damaged mixed-language titles such as `La Crédulité des incré不信者的轻信（dules）`, setup paragraphs whose promised verse sits in another unit, flattened verse quotations, false-negative constructions such as `并不比任何别的人更不可接受`, raw italic index headwords such as `_lady-like_ woman`, `_law_`, or `_Sartum tectum_`, and source titles split across line breaks. Scan exact sampled strings first, then related `_[A-Za-z][^_]{2,}_` index residues and setup endings before deciding. Repair with a Chinese reader label first, source form in parentheses when helpful, XHTML-safe poem blocks for verse, and sampler smoke tests that prove the failed bracket note is no longer sampled as a paragraph. Rebuild and open a fresh seed because sampler or text repairs keep the round out of the clean PASS streak.
- `preservative` polysemy guard: In doctrinal or polemical contexts, `preservative` often means a protective formula, antidote, or preventive treatise, not a literal `防腐剂`. Scan `防腐剂|preservative|antidote|remedy|防腐` and repair only confirmed doctrinal or polemical false friends; keep real body-preservation or embalming contexts as exceptions.
  引文释义桥防护：源语引文可以保留简短原文片段，但目标语释义必须读成完成态注释。应在终稿、译稿、生成 XHTML 和抽样 manifest 中扫描 `：意思是：`、` 意思是：`、`说：意思是：`、`表达 意思是` 等制作痕迹；确认命中后改成 `其意为：`、直接引语或正常书目句。`这句话的意思是` 这类普通表述，只有在整条读者单元已经流畅可懂时才作为合理例外保留。

When a concrete book discovers a new quality family, write the immediate evidence in that book's `retrospective_lessons.md`, `qa/chapter_controls/`, or `reviews/random_spotcheck/round_XXX/fixes/fix_log.md`, then backfill the reusable lesson here.
具体书发现新的质量问题族时，先把即时证据写入该书的 `retrospective_lessons.md`、`qa/chapter_controls/` 或 `reviews/random_spotcheck/round_XXX/fixes/fix_log.md`，再把可复用经验回填到本 skill。

Backfill only the general lesson: how to find it, how to classify it, how to audit similar cases, how to fix it, and how to recheck it.
回填只写通用经验：如何发现、如何归纳、如何审计同类、如何修复、如何复查。

### Round 390 hard-translation and reader-unit closure addendum

When samples repeatedly expose source-order calques, metaphor literalization, missing subjects, unit wording, or commentary inserted between narrative clauses, first build low-token candidate pools from the sampled strings, glossary, source/final pairs, and note markers. Confirm each hit against the source, then audit the whole book for the same family before editing. Keep source-backed dual note references as explicit exceptions; do not merge them merely because their markers are adjacent. For setup lists and quotations, preserve translation-coverage paragraph blocks while making the reader-facing unit self-contained; if a merge lowers structural coverage, restore paragraph boundaries and use a concise date or payload label rather than deleting content. Rebuild, rerun coverage/publication/reader/EPUB gates, and open a fresh seed; repaired rounds never count toward the clean PASS streak.

### Domain Metaphor False Literalization / 领域隐喻误作字面动作

- Symptom: A source term from bodily process, craft, cookery, medicine, law, or another technical domain is used metaphorically in criticism or anecdote, but the target text turns it into a literal physical action. This creates a comic or misleading image not present in the source, such as treating dramatic `concoction` as digestion rather than initial composition.
  症状：源语把身体过程、工艺、烹调、医学、法律等领域词借入批评或轶事语境，译文却把它落成字面动作，造成原文没有的滑稽或误导图像。例如戏剧评论里的 `concoction` 应指最初构思，不应变成消化过程。
- Find by: Search exact source triggers and repaired target candidates together. Useful patterns include `concoction|first concoction|irons in the fire|Quare adhaesit pavimento|pavimento` plus target candidates such as `第一道消化|调制|最初构思|铁在火里|人行道`. Inspect the sentence domain before deciding whether the image is literal, idiomatic, legal, or a source-quote interface.
  发现方式：源语触发词和译文候选并查。可用模式包括 `concoction|first concoction|irons in the fire|Quare adhaesit pavimento|pavimento`，并联查 `第一道消化|调制|最初构思|铁在火里|人行道` 等译文候选。判定前先看句子领域，区分字面图像、习语、法律笑话和源语引文接口。
- Evaluative-verb extension: Historical notes often use `swallowed`, `embraced`, or `espoused` for credulous acceptance of a claim. Scan target residues such as `贪婪吞下|吞下.*说法|吞下.*指控|急切吞下`; if the object is a thesis, calumny, tradition, or opinion, translate the evaluative relation as `急切采信`, `轻信接受`, `照单全收`, or `偏执地采纳`. Record literal eating or birds/beasts devouring bodies as exceptions.
  评价动词扩展：历史注释常用 `swallowed`、`embraced`、`espoused` 表示轻信或采信某种说法。可扫目标语残留 `贪婪吞下|吞下.*说法|吞下.*指控|急切吞下`；若宾语是论点、诽谤、传统或意见，应译成“急切采信”“轻信接受”“照单全收”“偏执地采纳”等评价关系。真正吃食、鸟兽吞食尸体等字面场景应记录为例外。
- Sacred or classical image extension: Terms like `fiery chariot` belong to biblical or classical imagery, not modern transport. Scan source triggers such as `fiery chariot` with target candidates `火车|火焰车|火焰战车`; in modern Chinese, prefer `火焰战车` or another context-bound image, and record literal modern vehicles as defects unless the source scene is actually modern.
  圣经或古典意象扩展：`fiery chariot` 这类词属于圣经或古典意象，不是现代交通工具。并查源文触发词 `fiery chariot` 与译文候选 `火车|火焰车|火焰战车`；现代中文通常应译作 `火焰战车` 或其他贴合语境的意象，除非源文场景本身是现代交通，否则现代车辆字面义应视为缺陷。
- Fix by: Translate the function in the active domain first. For dramatic judgement, use `最初构思` or another composition term; for a joke that intentionally literalizes an idiom, keep the literal image only if the Chinese sentence also makes the joke intelligible. For short Latin legal phrases, keep the Latin when it carries the joke or evidence, but add an immediate Chinese meaning.
  修复方式：先按当前语域翻译功能。戏剧判断用 `最初构思` 等写作/构作术语；若原文故意把习语字面化，则仅在中文也能读出笑点时保留字面图像。短拉丁法律语若承担笑点或证据，可保留拉丁原文，但附近必须有中文含义接口。
- Recheck: A round that fixes this family cannot PASS. Rerun a fresh whole-unit review, then rescan both the rejected literal wording and the accepted domain term in translated drafts, final Markdown, generated XHTML when available, glossary rows, and QA notes.

### Index mixed-language fallback and context-map contamination

- Symptom: An index headword is rendered as a partial mixture such as `sleep, 大脑 activity during`, or a context-reuse rule copies a whole explanatory sentence into a short index entry. This is a reader-facing translation defect even when the original headword is retained in parentheses for traceability.
- Find by: Audit every `chapters/final/026_index.md` target headword before its source-parenthesis boundary. Reject lowercase source-language words outside intentional acronyms or proper-name witnesses, reject unusually long sentence-like headwords, and exclude the index itself when building reusable `target（source）` context mappings.
- Recurrence guard: Context reuse must also reject captures containing sentence punctuation or overlong target spans; when a source index entry is `Surname, Given name`, try the reversed body witness `Given name Surname` without copying surrounding prose. Audit cross-reference labels after page locators and allow only documented acronyms, model names, and source witnesses to remain in Latin script.
  复发防护：上下文复用还必须拒绝含句末标点或过长目标片段的捕获；源索引采用 `姓, 名` 时，应尝试匹配正文中的 `名 姓`，但不能把前置整句复制进去。页码定位符之后的交叉参照也要继续审计；只有有记录的缩写、模型名和原文见证可以保留拉丁字母。
- Fix by: Translate the complete headword with phrase-level mappings first, then conservative token fallbacks; keep the original headword only in the following parenthetical witness. For cross-references, translate the referenced headword as well. Keep glossary headings target-language-only and move the source term into a body note.
- Recheck: Audit all index lines, bibliography/glossary boundaries, final and translated Markdown, regenerated XHTML, publication lint, translation coverage, chapter controls, bilingual checks, and EPUBCheck. A repaired round remains out of the clean PASS streak and requires a fresh seed.
  复查：凡修复此问题族的轮次不能 PASS。必须重新做一轮整章复查，并在译稿、终稿、已生成 XHTML（如已有）、术语表和 QA 记录中同时扫描被拒译法与已采纳的领域术语。

### Reader-facing Markdown structure, figure alt, and bibliography spacing / 读者可见 Markdown 结构、图像替代文本与书目间距

- Figure axis and table-note interface: Captions that describe axis labels must use the book's locked term (Delta → `德尔塔`, or the symbol `Δ` where the locked literal cannot be written outside a placeholder), never an invented paraphrase such as `价格敏感度` that contradicts the body. When a table survives only as a source image, its footnote marks (`*`, `e`) need a Chinese table note (`表注：……`), or readers cannot decode `6e` and `30*`.
  图轴与表注接口：图注描述坐标轴时，必须使用书内锁定术语（Delta 译“德尔塔”；锁定字面无法写在占位符外时用符号“Δ”），不得自造与正文矛盾的说法，例如“价格敏感度”。表格只以原书图片保留时，图中的脚注符号（`*`、`e`）必须给出中文表注（“表注：……”），否则读者看不懂“6e”“30*”。
- Figure-text transcription with locked names: When a caption says `图中文字：…`, it must transcribe what the image actually prints. Do not turn `XYZ: 82` into `该股票：82`, `Delta` into `Δ`, or `PERCS` into a generic `优先股`. If the unit already holds the needed `{{pn:...}}` placeholders, move them into the transcription without changing their order, give the caption title a neutral wording, and put the full names in the `alt` attribute (attributes are outside the locked-literal scan). If the unit has no placeholder for the name, use a non-misleading back-reference (`正文所述优先股`), record a contract exception, and keep the full name in `alt`.
  含锁定名的图中文字转写：图注写“图中文字：……”时，必须忠实转写图上实际印的文字，不能把 `XYZ: 82` 改成“该股票：82”，把 `Delta` 改成“Δ”，或把 `PERCS` 泛化成“优先股”。本单元已有所需 `{{pn:...}}` 占位符时，在不改变顺序的前提下把它们移入转写处，图题改用中性写法，完整名称写进 `alt` 属性（属性不在锁定字面扫描范围内）。本单元没有对应占位符时，用不会误导的回指（如“正文所述优先股”），记录合同例外，并在 `alt` 中保留全称。

- Aggregate-caption and punctuation guard: Review a collage's overall category independently of its panel objects, relations and printed labels. Correct individual objects do not cure an unsupported or omitted aggregate category; sweep every caption sharing that source category, including earlier accepted captions. Distinguish added editorial image descriptions from original author captions. Separately record format-only countdown or ellipsis spacing changes; improving typography must not be counted as a semantic repair or trigger indiscriminate replacement of every dot pattern.
  整体图注与标点防护：拼图整体类别须与分图对象、关系及印字分别审查；对象准确不能弥补整体类别的新增或遗漏。新发现类别问题后，应重查同类全部图注，包括此前接受项；编辑增补图像描述与作者原图注须区分。倒计时或省略号留空等纯格式变更单列，不能冒称语义修复，也不能因此机械替换全书点号。


- Symptom: Markdown list markers or blockquote markers become visible text in XHTML, a table is labelled as a figure, a complex diagram has only `图像` or a figure number as its `alt`, or bibliography/index punctuation retains source-side spacing such as `Title:Word`, `MA:MIT`, `； repr.`, `59 – 60 , 63 – 64`. These are reader-facing interface defects even when the underlying source facts are correct.
  症状：Markdown 列表符号或引用符号在 XHTML 中变成可见正文，表格被标成图，复杂图只使用 `图像` 或图号作为 `alt`，或者书目/索引保留 `Title:Word`、`MA:MIT`、`； repr.`、`59 – 60 , 63 – 64` 这类源式间距。即使源文事实没有错，这些仍是读者可见接口缺陷。
- Find by: Scan `chapters/final`, `chapters/translated`, and generated XHTML for `^[-*] `, `^> `, generic image alts, `tblu` assets labelled `图`, `:\S`, `\s+[–—]\s+`, and spaces before commas. Inspect only the matched Markdown block and its source/image evidence; do not flatten a list or quote merely to make a sampler pass.
  发现方式：在 `chapters/final`、`chapters/translated` 和生成 XHTML 中扫描 `^[-*] `、`^> `、通用图像 alt、被标为“图”的 `tblu` 资产、`:\S`、`\s+[–—]\s+` 以及逗号前空格。只对命中的 Markdown 块及其原文/图像证据读小上下文；不能为了让抽样通过而把列表或引文展平。
- Fix by: Render ordered and unordered lists as semantic `<ol>/<ul><li>` and quoted prose as `<blockquote>`; give tables their table number and a content-bearing `alt`/caption; describe the visible variables, paths, relations, or medical/experimental content of complex figures; normalize bibliography colons, semicolons, apostrophes, and index page ranges while preserving author, title, DOI, and page facts.
  修复方式：把有序/无序列表渲染为语义化 `<ol>/<ul><li>`，把引文渲染为 `<blockquote>`；表格使用正确表号并提供包含内容的 `alt`/图注；复杂图的替代文本应说明可见变量、路径、关系或医学/实验内容；统一书目冒号、分号、撇号和索引页码范围的间距，同时保留作者、题名、DOI 和页码事实。
- Author-connector guard: In Chinese reader-facing prose, a Latin-script author witness should not leave an English `and` as a mixed-language connective when the surrounding citation is otherwise Chinese. Normalize confirmed author connectors to `与`, but preserve English book-title phrases, formulas, and bibliography-appendix records as documented exceptions. Start with `rg -n -F " and " chapters/final chapters/translated`, classify title/formula/appendix exceptions, and rescan generated XHTML after rebuilding.
  作者连接词防护：中文读者可见正文中，若周围引文已经是中文接口，拉丁字母作者见证不应继续裸留英文 `and` 作为混合连接词。确认属于正文作者连接词的命中统一改为 `与`；英文书名、公式和书目附录原始记录保留为合理例外并记录。先用 `rg -n -F " and " chapters/final chapters/translated` 建候选池，分类书名/公式/附录例外，重建后再扫描生成 XHTML。
- Recheck: Rebuild the EPUB, inspect representative XHTML for `<ul>`, `<ol>`, `<blockquote>`, table labels, and non-generic `alt` values, then run publication lint, asset lint, translation coverage, chapter controls, bilingual checks, and EPUBCheck. A round with these repairs is an issue round and cannot count toward the consecutive clean PASS streak.
  复查：重建 EPUB，抽查代表性 XHTML 是否出现 `<ul>`、`<ol>`、`<blockquote>`、正确表号和非通用 `alt`，再运行出版检查、资产检查、翻译覆盖、章节控制、双语检查和 EPUBCheck。包含此类修复的轮次属于问题轮，不能计入连续干净 PASS 轮次。

### Repeated lexical rendering and duplicated Chinese spans

- Appositive-description guard: Repeating a source noun across an inserted explanation may produce several target actions for one event. Merge `lights ... lights not noticed before ... green` into one activation with its descriptive qualification and color. Do not collapse genuinely distinct objects, locations or functions, such as separate yellow and violet lights. Search the known noun and nearby repetitions, then decide with complete source and neighbors.
  同位描述防护：把插入说明中的同一名词再次译出，可能使一个事件变成多次中文动作。`lights ... lights not noticed before ... green` 可合并成此前未注意到的灯一次亮起并发出绿光。不同物体、位置或功能仍须分别保留，例如用途不同的黄光与紫光。先检索已知名词及邻近重复，再按完整源句和邻段判定。

- Symptom: A source concept is rendered twice inside one target span, producing forms such as `搜索搜索空间`, `数量的数量`, `自然选择选择了`, `表征表征`, or `初级视觉皮层皮层输出`. These defects can remain semantically guessable while making the textbook sound mechanically generated.
  症状：同一源概念在一个中文片段中被重复译出，例如 `搜索搜索空间`、`数量的数量`、`自然选择选择了`、`表征表征` 或 `初级视觉皮层皮层输出`。这类问题即使不妨碍猜义，也会使教材呈现机械生成痕迹。
- Find by: Build a low-token candidate pool with `rg --pcre2 "(\p{Han}{2,5})\1" chapters/final chapters/translated`, plus exact scans for known duplicated terms and glossary target variants. Inspect each hit against the full source sentence because legitimate Chinese reduplication, quoted wording, and proper names are exceptions.
  发现方式：先用 `rg --pcre2 "(\p{Han}{2,5})\1" chapters/final chapters/translated`，再结合已知重复术语和术语表目标词做低 token 候选扫描。每个命中都要回看完整源句；合法的汉语叠词、引文措辞和专名属于例外。
- Fix by: Keep one target rendering and restore the source relation, grammar, and aspect rather than deleting tokens blindly. Record the confirmed family examples and their chapter locations, then widen the scan book-wide before applying mechanical replacements.
  修复方式：保留一个目标语译法，并恢复源句中的关系、语法和体貌，不得盲删词语。记录已确认的例子及章节位置，先把扫描扩大到全书，再做机械替换。
- Recheck: A repair round for this family is `FIXED_RECHECK_REQUIRED`, never PASS. Re-run full-chapter source/translation/context comparison, the duplicate scan, terminology forbidden-rendering scan, regenerated XHTML scan, publication and EPUB gates, and a fresh random seed; only the later zero-issue round may pass.
  复查：修复此问题族的轮次必须记为 `FIXED_RECHECK_REQUIRED`，不能记 PASS。随后重新逐章对照原文、译文和上下文，重跑重复词扫描、术语禁用写法扫描、生成 XHTML 扫描、出版与 EPUB 门禁，并开启新随机种子；只有之后的零问题轮次才可通过。
### Technical-term semantic drift and literal-calque polish / 技术术语语义漂移与直译腔润色

- Data-object and guarantee guard: Separate a physical chip, its extraction, and training data obtained from a battle; a chip used to obtain data is not itself the data. Resolve object ellipses such as a drone swarm coming to `finish ours off` from the local referent before making the target appear to describe killing people. `Built-in hard guarantees` about information transmission must retain the device's strict can/cannot boundary when redundant noun scaffolding is removed; do not reduce it to an unenforced statement, or invent an unspecified hardware/software implementation. Compare the full technical claim, its uncertainty markers, and neighboring explanation before rewriting.
  数据对象与严格保障防护：区分实体芯片、提取芯片的动作与从战斗取得的训练数据，用来获取数据的芯片并非数据本身。无人机群赶来finish ours off等对象省略须先按局部指代明确我方无人机，避免让译文显得是在消灭人员。删去built-in hard guarantees的重复名词支架时，仍须保留设备内置、严格的可/不可传输边界，不能弱化为没有执行保障的说法，也不新增未明确的硬件或软件实现。改写前核对完整技术论断、不确定标记及邻文解释。

- Process/type guard: A technical verb must fit the local process. Information loss described as `entropy collapses` need not be a quantum-state collapse; `re-randomized AI-generated voice` describes randomization of a generated voice, not necessarily regenerating it. In algorithm prose, determine what a final modifier such as `added to the end` attaches to before inventing a result-appending step. Compare complete source/target and neighbors, preserve the author's claim and any documented ambiguity, and record genuine physical randomization or literal appending as contextual exceptions.
  过程与类型防护：技术动词须符合局部过程。信息损失语境的entropy collapses不一定是量子态塌缩；re-randomized AI-generated voice指合成声音再经随机化，不必意味着重新生成声音。算法说明中须先判定added to the end等句末修饰语的附着对象，不能擅添拼接结果步骤。回读完整源译及邻文，保留作者论断和已记录歧义，真实的物理随机化或末尾拼接应记作语境例外。

- Predicate-relation guard: A correct technical noun can still occur in a wrong relation. `checking that the tax-paying transaction type was used` asks whether payment used a specified transaction type, not whether that type belongs to a tax regime. Back-check the predicate, its subject/object, conditions and interface witnesses together; a terminology string match is insufficient.
  谓词关系防护：技术名词正确，句中关系仍可能错误。`checking that the tax-paying transaction type was used` 核验付款是否使用规定的缴税交易类型，不是核验该类型是否纳入税制。应连同谓词、主宾语、条件及界面见证一起回查，不能只靠术语字符串相同判断正确。

- Symptom: A technical term is translated through a false friend or a mechanically literal phrase, or an otherwise accurate sentence keeps a stiff image that makes the Chinese reader pause. In this book the confirmed examples were `null hypothesis` rendered as `无效假设`, `beating them into submission by sheer computational power` rendered as `把问题压服`, `a unified intellectual enterprise` rendered as `统一的智识事业`, and `biological plausibility ... goes even deeper` rendered as `生物学合理性还更深入`.
  症状：技术术语被假朋友或机械直译带偏，或者句意虽大体正确，却保留让中文读者停顿的生硬意象。本书确认的例子包括：`null hypothesis` 译成“无效假设”、`beating them into submission by sheer computational power` 译成“把问题压服”、`a unified intellectual enterprise` 译成“统一的智识事业”，以及 `biological plausibility ... goes even deeper` 译成“生物学合理性还更深入”。
- Find by: Start with reviewer-confirmed source/target pairs, then scan the whole book for the rejected target phrase, the source technical term, glossary witnesses, and nearby variants. Verify the term in its disciplinary context before changing it; distinguish a genuine statistical `null hypothesis` from ordinary uses of “假设”, and distinguish a deliberate metaphor from an accidental literal calque.
  发现方式：先从 reviewer 确认的原文—译文对开始，再用被否定的译文短语、源语技术术语、术语表见证和邻近变体扫描全书。修改前必须回到学科语境核对；要区分统计学中的 `null hypothesis` 与一般“假设”，也要区分原文有意使用的比喻和无意形成的字面直译。
- Fix by: Choose the established target term (`零假设` here), then rewrite the surrounding sentence into idiomatic Chinese while preserving the source claim and rhetorical force. Keep terminology and name strategy consistent in final, translated, glossary, index, alignment, and generated EPUB text.
  修复方式：优先采用规范目标术语（本书为“零假设”），再把周围句子改写成自然中文，同时保留原文论断和修辞力度；并在终稿、译稿、术语表、索引、双语映射和生成 EPUB 文本中保持术语与专名策略一致。
- Scope extension: Scientific-interface defects also include a technically plausible but nonstandard collocation, a word-order calque that changes a relation, and a source-language author label left bare in Chinese prose. Confirmed examples included `psychological constants` rendered as “心理恒常性” rather than “心理上的稳定联系”, `interocular suppression` rendered as “双眼抑制” rather than a hybrid with “双眼竞争”, `frequency of correlations` rendered as “相关模式出现的频率”, and “自由度数目极其庞大”. Strategy-3 prose references should introduce a Chinese name before the original witness, for example `布洛克（Block，1981）`, instead of leaving `Block（1981）` bare.
  范围扩展：科学接口问题还包括“技术上似乎说得通”但并非规范搭配的译法、改变关系结构的源语词序直译，以及中文正文中裸留的源语作者名。本轮确认的例子包括：`psychological constants` 应译为“心理恒常性”而不是“心理上的稳定联系”；`interocular suppression` 应译为“双眼抑制”，不能与“双眼竞争”混杂；`frequency of correlations` 应译为“相关模式出现的频率”；“自由度数目极其庞大”也需要经过中文搭配检查。策略 3 的正文引文应先给中文姓名、再给原文见证，例如 `布洛克（Block，1981）`，不能裸留 `Block（1981）`。
- Find efficiently: Use the glossary and reviewer-confirmed source/target pairs to seed exact scans, then search source terms and nearby target variants across translated Markdown, final Markdown, glossary, index, and generated XHTML. For names, scan prose for Latin-script surname-plus-year patterns and verify whether a Chinese-first interface exists; for technical terms, compare the local sentence against the term's glossary definition and neighboring contrast terms.
  高效发现：用术语表和 reviewer 确认的原文—译文对建立精确扫描种子，再把源术语及其目标语邻近变体扩展到译稿、终稿、术语表、索引和生成 XHTML。专名方面，扫描正文中的“拉丁字母姓氏＋年份”模式，并核对是否已有中文优先接口；技术术语方面，将当前句与术语表定义及相邻对比术语一起核对。
- Recheck: Record the confirmed family in the book QA before declaring the repaired round complete. Rerun the full-chapter source/translation/context review, exact rejected-string scans, terminology checks, translation coverage, publication and EPUB gates, and a fresh random seed. A repaired random-review round is `FIXED_RECHECK_REQUIRED`; only a later complete zero-issue round can count as PASS.
  复查：在宣告修复轮完成前，先把已确认问题族记录到本书 QA。重新执行逐章原文—译文—上下文复核、被否定短语精确扫描、术语检查、翻译覆盖、出版和 EPUB 门禁，并使用新的随机种子。发生修复的随机抽检轮必须记为 `FIXED_RECHECK_REQUIRED`；只有之后完整的零问题轮次才能计为 PASS。

### Intentional Clumsiness, Invented Gender, Reversed Negation and In-World System Drift / 刻意笨拙被润顺、性别增补、否定反转与世界观体系漂移

- Intentional-clumsiness guard: When a learner or non-native speaker says something over-literal and the next speaker corrects it (`earth sheet of green plants` → `Landscape`, `without an upper lid` → `Unlimited`, `stopped behind` → `left behind`), the target must stay equally clumsy so the correction still has an object. Smoothing the first line into fluent prose deletes the joke and makes the correction meaningless. Find by scanning for short correction replies (`X, <name> corrected`, quoted one-word replies) and reading the preceding line; repair by literalizing the learner line, never the correction.
  刻意笨拙防护：学习者或非母语角色说出逐字直译的笨拙句、下一位说话人随即纠正时（如“绿色植物的地皮”→“景观”、“没有上面的盖子”→“无限”、“停在了后面”→“被抛在了后面”），译文必须同样笨拙，纠正才有对象。把前句润成通顺中文会删掉笑点并让纠正失去意义。发现方式：扫描简短纠正回复（“某某纠正道”、单词引语）并回读前一句；修复时只把笨拙句直译化，不改纠正句。
- Invented-gender guard: Do not add a gendered pronoun where the source has none. A Chinese 她/他 inserted for `completely uninjured` turned a male character female. Check every inserted third-person pronoun against the character's established gender elsewhere in the book.
  性别增补防护：原文无代词时不得自行补出带性别的代词。把 `completely uninjured` 补译为“她毫发无伤”使男性角色变成女性。凡增补的第三人称代词都要对照全书人物性别核验。
- Reversed-negation guard: Scan for stacked negatives such as `不是什么不太`, `并非不`, `不是不` and compare with the source; `not a very polite plan` became “不是什么不太客气的计划”, reversing the meaning.
  否定反转防护：扫描“不是什么不太”“并非不”“不是不”等叠加否定并回查原文；`not a very polite plan` 曾被译成“不是什么不太客气的计划”，意思完全反转。
- Fictional-language guard: Short strings of an invented language (`ten min` in a device header) can look like English and get translated (“十分钟”). Confirm the token belongs to the invented-language lexicon before translating any short Latin-script UI text.
  虚构语言防护：虚构语言的短串（设备表头 `ten min`）可能被误当英文译成“十分钟”。翻译界面上的短拉丁字母文本前，先核对它是否属于虚构语言词表。
- In-world system and format drift: One in-world concept must keep one rendering across chapters: reputation score/reputation (one pair, e.g. 声望分/声望), co-governance (共治), named reports, game terms (qualifying, rock formation), and `City, Country` dateline order and date format. Spatial phrases such as `sixteen streets ... apart from north to south` describe arrangement, not street direction — avoid “南北向十六条街道”. Find by grouping all units whose source contains the system term and tabulating target variants; fix to the majority or the more accurate rendering and record the decision.
  世界观体系与格式漂移：同一世界观概念在全书只能有一种译法：声誉分数/声誉（一对固定译法，如“声望分/声望”）、co-governance（共治）、具名报告、游戏术语（预选赛、岩石构造），以及日期行“城市，国家”的顺序与日期格式。`sixteen streets ... apart from north to south` 讲的是排列方向而非街道走向，不能译成“南北向十六条街道”。发现方式：把源文含该体系术语的所有单元汇总，统计译法变体；按多数或更准确的译法统一，并记录裁决。
- In-world UI label drift: repeated interface labels (message-table header `From`, device status `Attested`, process nouns such as `audits`) must share one rendering across chapters (here 发件人 / 已认证 / 审计). Scan every unit whose source contains the label and tabulate the target variants before the final random spot-check; a single stray variant is a family, not a one-off.
  世界观界面标签漂移：反复出现的界面标签（消息表头 `From`、设备状态 `Attested`、`audits` 等流程名词）全书只能有一种译法（本书为“发件人”“已认证”“审计”）。最终随机抽检前，应汇总源文含该标签的所有单元并统计译法变体；哪怕只有零星异译，也按问题族处理。
- Recheck: these repairs are `FIXED_RECHECK_REQUIRED`; every touched chapter needs a fresh zero-issue full-chapter round, and a family fix applied to an already sealed chapter re-opens that chapter.
  复查：这些修复都属于 `FIXED_RECHECK_REQUIRED`；凡被改动的章节都需要新的零问题整章复核，问题族修复若触及已封存章节，该章即重新打开。

### Silently normalised source typos / 源文排印错误被静默改写（第7轮回填）

- Symptom: a source word is misspelled ("Black-Schoes", "artibrageur", "voltility"). The translation silently writes the correct formal name or word with no note, so the reader cannot check the original. Treat as a missing translator note.
- Fix: translate the intended name or word, then add one 译注 in the same chapter stating the printed form, the correction, and the rendering used (e.g. 原文作X，系Y之误，此处按Z译出). Do not write a locked name literally inside the note; use its placeholder only if the occurrence ledger expects it.
- Guard: a note must not add placeholder occurrences the ledger does not expect, and must not put a locked literal outside its placeholder.
- Backfill record: round 007 (book 3 期权战略投资), units 0e8d45f5, c7a05899, 55ad1559.

### Figure alt-text families, note-marker audits and register-name disputes / 图片说明家族、注号核对与登记译名争议（头寸规模确定书，抽检第1–11轮回填）

- Alt-text families: when a figure alt is rewritten, treat every sibling figure of the same template as one family and fix them together (e.g. optimizer-table row labels must match the book's transcription table: 最高平均收益 / 最高中位收益 / 破产概率<1% / 破产概率>0% / 达标概率减破产概率; a label followed directly by a number needs a connector such as “为”; distribution panels must describe what the image really shows — blank plot area with the printed banner, headings versus buttons). Verify every number against the figure's data CSV.
  图片说明家族：改写一个图片 alt 时，把同模板的全部同类图当作一个问题族一起修，并逐项对照图的数据 CSV；行标签与转录表保持一致，标签后紧跟数字时加连接词，描述必须与图像实际内容一致。
- XML escaping: `<` and `>` inside an `alt` attribute must be written `&lt;` / `&gt;`; a raw bracket makes the raw XHTML container invalid and breaks the build.
  XML 转义：alt 属性里的 `<`、`>` 必须写成 `&lt;`、`&gt;`，否则原始 XHTML 不合法、构建失败。
- Note-marker audits: a reviewer claim of a "dangling" `[^n]` must be checked against `chapters/notes/{chapter}.md` (plain `[n] text` definitions) before any fix; translator-note anchors that the source lacks are by design. Four rounds of P1 claims were misreads.
  注号核对：评审称注号“悬空”时，先查 `chapters/notes/{chapter}.md` 的 `[n] 文本` 定义再决定是否修改；译者注可以新增源文没有的锚点，历轮 P1 多为误读。
- Register-name disputes: product and company names rendered by the locked proper-noun register (e.g. the TradeSim descriptive rendering) are policy decisions; a spot-check reviewer disagreeing with a documented register rendering is recorded as a dispute and closed with the register evidence, not by local edits that break the ledger.
  登记译名争议：专名登记表锁定的软件和公司译名属于项目决定；评审对已记录的登记译名有异议时，按争议记录并以登记表证据关闭，不得用局部改写破坏占用台账。
- Polish limit: after a whole-book polish pass (149 units changed across 8 chapter groups, rechecked), spot-check averages stayed near 90–91; remaining score loss is spread over isolated stiff sentences and register-driven repeated full names, not a systematic style defect.
  精修上限：全书精修（8 组、149 单元）后抽检均分仍约 90–91，剩余扣分分散在个别生硬句和登记术语重复全称，不再是系统性文笔问题。
- Backfill record: book 4 头寸规模确定权威指南, spot-check rounds 001–011.
- Locked-name captions and alt text (options book, rounds 019–030): when a figure or caption unit carries no `{{pn:...}}` placeholder, writing the locked Chinese form (e.g. a Greek-letter name, a product full name) or the English full name in it is rejected (`CSV_ONLY_RENDER_REQUIRED`), and adding a placeholder is rejected too (`ENTITY_OCCURRENCE_COVERAGE_FAILED`). Reword without the locked form (e.g. "图题所示指标", "时间损耗") or, if the placeholder count already matches the source caption, move the placeholder into the title; otherwise record a contract exception with the evidence instead of retrying edits. Never delete a placeholder to "fix" wording.
  锁定名与图注、alt：图注或 alt 单元没有占位符时，直接写锁定中文名或英文全称会被拒绝，新增占位符也会被拒绝。应改用不含锁定形式的写法；若占位符数量与源图题一致，可把占位符移到图题；否则记录合同例外并附证据，不要反复试改，更不要为改措辞删除占位符。
- Terminology unification by whole-book search: one unit used a synonym for a term fixed elsewhere (e.g. a "保本价" family against the book-wide "盈亏平衡点"). Search the whole reader-facing book for the synonym, replace the noun uses in every unit in one batch, and keep verb uses.
  术语统一：同一术语在个别章节用了近义词时，全书检索该近义词，一次批量替换所有名词用法，动词用法保留。
- Cross-reference titles: a reference such as "第3章题为“…”的一节" must reuse the exact chapter heading in the final text; check it against the heading, not against memory.
  交叉引用题名：“第 N 章题为“…”的一节”必须与成稿标题一字不差，按标题核对。
- Note anchors: every `译注[n]：` needs exactly one body anchor `[n]` (or a `sup` anchor in a figure caption) in the same unit, and notes are numbered in reading order within the chapter. Count `[n]` occurrences per chapter (each number must appear twice: anchor and label) after any note edit.
  译注锚点：每条“译注[n]：”在同一单元内必须有且只有一个正文锚点，章内按阅读顺序编号；改动译注后按章统计 [n] 出现次数（锚点和注文各一次）。
- Reviewer misalignment: a spot-check claim of "added", "omitted" or "reversed" must be checked against the unit's own `source_text` before any edit. Several severe claims in rounds 017–022 and 026 were sample-to-source misalignments; the translation followed the source, and editing it (or deleting a faithful sentence) broke the occurrence ledger.
  评审错配：评审称“增译、漏译、结论相反”时，先对照该单元自己的 `source_text` 再改；多条“严重”实为样本与源段错配，译文本忠实，盲目修改还会破坏占用台账。
- Semicolons in new notes: a newly written translator note or list line containing `；` fails the semicolon gate (F33 UNRESOLVED); use a comma or a full stop.
  新写的译注或列表行不要用中文分号，否则分号门禁失败，改用逗号或句号。
- Backfill record: options book 期权战略投资, spot-check rounds 008–030.
- Translator notes must state only verified facts (options book, round 034): a note that says the book has a printing error must be recomputed with that unit's own numbers and premises. One note applied the formula of the previous worked example to a different position and called a correct figure an error; another gave a "corrected solution" that did not satisfy the printed equations; another called a normal debit-spread quote a mistake. Do not carry numbers across examples, do not state causes you cannot verify (old edition, OCR), and when the source phrasing is a standard usage, add no note. After finding one bad note, audit every note in the book with independent recomputation.
  译注只写已核实内容：声称原书有误的译注必须用该单元自己的数字和前提复算；不得把前一个例子的算式套到另一个头寸，不得写无法证实的原因（旧版文字、识别错误），原文是通行说法时不加译注；发现一条错注后，要对全书译注做独立复算审计。
- Doubled-character typos (options book, round 036): search the book for the exact suspicious string (e.g. "即即") and fix every hit in one batch; a generic repeated-character regex matches hundreds of legitimate words and is not a usable audit.
  叠字笔误：评审指出一处“即即”后，用精确字符串检索全书并一次批量修正；宽泛的叠字正则会命中大量正常词语，不能作为审计手段。
- Registered-term placeholder collisions (position sizing book, round 016): a locked register term (e.g. "optimal bet size" rendered as 最优头寸规模) can collide with a neighbouring term of the same book (position size) and make a sentence contradict itself. The placeholder cannot be deleted (marker count must stay identical), so keep it and add a short appositive that disambiguates ("，也就是下注规模"), then recheck the unit independently. Do not touch the register for one site.
  登记术语占位符冲突（头寸规模确定书，抽检第16轮）：锁定登记词（如 optimal bet size 渲染为“最优头寸规模”）可能与同书相邻术语（头寸规模）混淆，使句子自相矛盾。占位符不能删（标记数必须一致），应保留并补一个消歧同位语（“，也就是下注规模”），再做独立复查；不要为单点改登记表。
- Backfill record: position sizing book 头寸规模确定, spot-check round 016.
- Edit only against the unit's own source (options book, round 038): before changing any sentence, read that translation unit's own `source_text`. A sentence that a reviewer calls "interpretive" may be a faithful rendering of this unit; a fix drawn from a similar sentence elsewhere in the chapter replaces fidelity with a different passage. One such edit made in round 021 was only caught by a random sample in round 038.
  只对照单元自己的源文修改：改任何句子前先读该翻译单元自己的 `source_text`；评审说“解释性改写”的句子可能正是本单元的忠实译文，拿章内相似句当依据会把忠实换成别处内容。第21轮的一次此类修改，直到第38轮才被随机抽到。

### Reviewer-fix truncation, scripted verdicts and gate collisions / 评审修订句截断、脚本代写判定与门禁冲突（雪月 v0.0.3，文学第9–36轮与抽检第10轮回填）

- Reviewer-suggested sentences can silently truncate a unit (snowmoon v0.0.3, literary round 11): of 55 suggested fixes, three replaced a multi-sentence unit with only its last sentence and one added a gender ("她") for a gender-neutral "a Keeper". The independent semantic review caught all four. Apply every suggested fix as an exact-substring replacement on the unit's own `target_template` (assert the pattern occurs exactly once), never by pasting the suggested sentence, and re-run the semantic review for every edited unit.
  评审给出的“建议句”可能把多句单元截成最后一句，或凭空加性别。一律按单元自己的 `target_template` 做精确子串替换（断言恰好命中一次），不要整句粘贴；改过的单元必须重新做语义复核。
- Scripted verdict files are not reviews: reviewer subagents wrote all-PASS files with one repeated summary through a script. Reject any results file whose summaries are identical or near-identical, quarantine it, and re-dispatch with an explicit "hand-written, unit-specific notes" instruction.
  脚本批量写 PASS 不是复核：摘要整章相同的结果文件一律作废并隔离，重派并明确要求逐条手写、摘要针对单元内容。
- Entity markers: a rewrite that drops one `{{pn:...}}` marker fails the merge (ENTITY_OCCURRENCE_COVERAGE_FAILED). Keep every marker, or rebuild the template from the rendered fix and check that the markers round-trip.
  改写丢掉一个实体标记会使合并失败；保留全部标记，或由渲染句反推模板并核对标记往返一致。
- Glossary and semicolon gates are keyed to exact text: a locked term whose forbidden rendering is a common word (XOR → 或者 forbidden) fires whenever that word appears in a unit whose source contains the term; an approved semicolon exception is keyed to the line's exact text, so any edit of that line needs the exception refreshed (or the semicolon removed, which is preferable).
  门禁按精确文本匹配：锁定术语的禁用译法若是常用词（XOR 禁用“或者”），含该术语的单元里一出现就报错；分号例外按整行文本登记，改动该行必须同步更新例外（更好的做法是直接去掉分号）。
- Silent-read variance: two independent silent readers flag different sentences each round, and single-reader flags rotate. Fix sentences both readers flag, name pronoun referents explicitly, split sentences that stack three actions, and state the scoring rubric (including that locked first-mention source-name glosses and setting-driven technical density are not by themselves deductions) identically to both readers.
  静读波动：两名读者每轮标记的句子不同；优先修两人同时标记的句子，明确代词指代，拆开叠三个动作的句子，并向两名读者用同一评分口径（锁定的首现原文括注、设定本身的技术词密度不单独扣分）。
- Packet text: build review packets with `get_text(" ")` then collapse spaces between CJK characters; `get_text("")` glues adjacent spans and produced false FAILs.
  审校包文本：用 `get_text(" ")` 再合并中文间空格；`get_text("")` 会粘连相邻 span，造成假 FAIL。
- Spot-check round 10 families: a figure title that named the same game differently from its sibling figures (圆点堆游戏 vs 取圆点游戏); straight single quotes around constructed-language words in dialogue (use nested curly quotes, search the whole book); a dropped size adjective ("oversized") in a noun phrase. Search the whole book for each family before resampling.
  抽检第10轮问题族：同一游戏在相邻图题里译名不一致；对话中构造语词用直单引号（改为弯引号并全书检索）；名词短语里漏掉尺寸形容词。重抽前全书检索各同类。
- Backfill record: snowmoon v0.0.3, literary rounds 9–36, spot-check round 010.
- Spot-check round 14 families (snowmoon v0.0.4): one caption of a four-figure group dropped a modifier its siblings carry (Drone-operation display → 行动界面 vs 无人机行动界面); one concept rendered two ways (casualty ratios as 损失比例 in three units, 伤亡比 in eight). When fixing a figure caption, change the svg `aria-label`, `<title>` and `<figcaption>` together; after any caption or term change, grep the whole unit store for the sibling captions and the variant rendering before resampling. Sampling exports flatten `<br/>` and cell borders into `||||`; check the real EPUB XHTML before calling it a table defect.
  抽检第14轮问题族：同组四张图题注有一张漏掉兄弟题注都有的修饰词；同一概念 casualty ratios 在三处译“损失比例”、八处译“伤亡比”。改图题注须同时改 svg 的 aria-label、title 与 figcaption；任何题注或术语改动后，重抽前全书检索同组题注与异译。抽检导出会把 `<br/>` 与单元格边界拍平成“||||”，判定表格缺陷前先看真实 EPUB XHTML。
- Spot-check round 17 family (snowmoon v0.0.4): one coined word (autobus) rendered 公共汽车 fifteen times and 自动公交 three times. When a reviewer flags one rendering of a recurring coined word, grep the whole unit store for every rendering of the source word, pick the majority, and unify before resampling. Also: if a reviewer misreads a locked ledger rendering (Sadzu Du → 主城) as a dropped name, tell the reviewer the ledger entry and let them rewrite their own row; never edit reviewer rows.
  抽检第17轮问题族：同一生造词 autobus 十五处译“公共汽车”、三处译“自动公交”。评审指出一处译法时，全书检索该原词的全部译法，取多数统一后再复抽。评审把已锁定的专名译法（Sadzu Du→主城）误读为漏译时，把账本条目告知评审并由其自行改写评审行，不得替评审改行。
- Options book spot-check round 41 families (private-use, finished-EPUB view): body text rendered italic and stray `*` leaked because the three builders each re-implement Markdown inline rules (builder-level, not unit-level: Chinese has no italics, so single `*x*`/`<em>` render as plain text, italics only for notes; unpaired OCR stars in the English source are removed in render AND in the reader-text normaliser of the checker, never by editing `chapters/src`); screen-reader text (`aria-label`, hidden table captions) must be stripped of markup; translator's computed notes must be recomputed by solving the equations (a note claimed 308/-186 by using the wrong right-hand side; the book's 243/-80 was right); in-figure redrawn SVGs must not repeat the printed title or use axis words different from the caption; `即例如` left over after bracket removal; the occurrence ledger fixes the exact count of locked-name placeholders per unit, so a rewrite may not add or drop one.
  期权战略投资抽检第41轮问题族：正文斜体和游离星号出自三个构建器各自实现的 Markdown 行内规则（构建器层问题，中文不用斜体，仅注释可用）；屏幕阅读文本也要去标记；译者计算型注释必须重新解方程核对（有一条注释把右边当成0算错，原书的 243/−80 才是对的）；重绘 SVG 不得重复图题或与图注用不同坐标轴词；括注拆除后残留“即例如”；出现次数账本固定了每个单元里锁定名占位符的数量，改写时不得增减。
- Backfill record: options book v0.0.3, spot-check round 041.
- Options book spot-check round 42 families: a stray translator's note on a sentence with no doubt (note numbering then runs out of order) and a note number printed twice; an original table footnote mislabelled as a translator's note; one source term carrying two renderings (ratio write 比例卖出 vs 按比例卖出; public customer 公众客户 vs 普通客户) — after one is flagged, sweep the whole unit store; a narrative sum that no longer matches the source arithmetic (10% − 90%×6% = 4.6%); an alt text that kept a whole English sentence. A "loss" on a stock in a short sale means the price fell, not that the position lost.
  期权战略投资抽检第42轮问题族：无疑点却多出的译注（注号乱序）与同一注号重复；原书表注被标成译注；同一原词两种译法（比例卖出/按比例卖出，公众客户/普通客户）：评审指出一处后全库检索统一；叙述中的算式与原文数字对不上；alt 里残留整句英文。卖空时股票的 loss 指价格下跌，不是头寸亏损。
- Options book spot-check round 43 families: a table cell that the source extraction dropped (Table 34-1 first-row price 0 left blank) — compare every cell of a table with the original table image, and when a defect is a dropped value search other tables for empty cells; a parenthetical glued into a sentence subject ("14天后的德尔塔，即同样见图…表明"); a pronoun with no antecedent at paragraph start. Do not write a locked name's literal Chinese form (瑞士) in a fix; reword around it.
  期权战略投资抽检第43轮问题族：表格抽取时丢失的单元格（表34-1首行价格 0 被留空）：表格须逐格对照原图，并全书检索空单元格；括注硬塞进主语的残句；段首无先行词的“他”。修改时不得直写锁定名的字面中文形式（瑞士），需绕开改写。
- Options book spot-check round 44 families: the same lone-zero-lost-in-extraction defect recurred in another table (Table 34 butterfly profit, 10 cells) after Table 34-1 was fixed — after the first hit, audit every table with empty cells against its page image in one pass (52 tables checked here; blank cells that are blank in print stay blank); reconciliation lines written as separate fragments with ASCII hyphens; "1%的½" for 0.5%; "below X in 6 months" is a point in time (6个月后), not a period.
  期权战略投资抽检第44轮问题族：表格丢零的同一缺陷在另一张表（蝶式价差盈利表，10格）再次出现：修完表34-1后应一次性对照原图审计所有带空单元格的表（本轮核对52张；原书本身留白的保持留白）；对账行被拆成碎句并用ASCII连字符；“1%的½”应写0.5%；“below X in 6 months”指时点（6个月后）而非期间。
- Options book spot-check round 45 family: OCR read the fraction ¼ as "%" ("parity about 17%"), the translation copied it and a translator's note then blamed the printed book. Before any note says "the original prints X / the original is wrong", render the original PDF page and look; scan sources for "number+%" in price/point contexts. Also: one source term with two renderings in a single chapter (比率卖方 vs 按比例卖出者).
  期权战略投资抽检第45轮问题族：OCR 把分数 ¼ 识别成“%”，译文照录，译注还把错误算到原书头上。写“原书印作/原书有误”之前必须渲染原书 PDF 页面核对；全书扫描源文中价格/点数语境的“数字+%”。另：同章同一术语两种译法（比率卖方/按比例卖出者）。
- Options book spot-check round 46 families: a printing smudge in the original (".6" for 6) copied as a wrong number into a formula — fix the number and explain in the note instead of copying the typo; "capture the negative tracking error" is favourable for a short seller, not "不利的"; terminology split inside one book (跨指数价差 vs 指数间价差; 若被行权的收益率 vs 被行权时的回报率) — after one hit, grep the unit store for every rendering; a figure caption that implies a curve that does not exist.
  期权战略投资抽检第46轮问题族：原书印刷污点（.6 应为 6）被照抄成错误数字：应改正数字并在注释说明；capture the negative tracking error 对卖空者有利，不是“不利的”；同书内术语分裂：命中一处后全库检索所有译法；图注暗示不存在的曲线。
- Options book spot-check round 47 families: table column markers (A)/(B) attached to the wrong columns because the extracted source was mis-aligned — check against the printed page, do not inherit the extraction; body text and translator's note contradicting each other about the same sentence ("高出110%以上" vs "高于110%"); a "× 5" cell in the wrong column; a locked name's literal form (欧洲美元, 德尔塔) cannot be written in a fix, so reword around it or accept the leftover.
  期权战略投资抽检第47轮问题族：表格列标记 (A)/(B) 因抽取错位挂错列：对照原书页面，不得沿用抽取结果；正文与译注对同一句话各说各话；“× 5”放错列；锁定名的字面形式不能写进修复，只能绕开改写或接受遗留。
- Options book spot-check round 48 family: "fair value" rendered 合理价值 in a dozen places while the glossary and 45 other places say 公允价值 — grep every rendering of a glossary term after one hit; a figure caption should not carry an explanatory parenthesis about why a code name was avoided.
  期权战略投资抽检第48轮问题族：fair value 在十余处译“合理价值”，而术语表和45处用“公允价值”：命中一处后检索术语表词条的所有译法；图注不要加“为何回避代码名”的解释性括注。
- Options book spot-check round 49 family: "X公司，即代码" repeated four times in one list; "one of the most influential" turned into the single "most influential" — keep "之一" quantifiers.
  期权战略投资抽检第49轮问题族：同一列举里“公司名，即代码”连用四次，统一为“公司名（代码）”；“one of the most…”不得丢掉“之一”。
- Options book spot-check round 50 families: a figure caption explained a "(!)" mark in the wrong direction ("下文计算所得" when the formula was above) — never guess what an original mark means, recompute; a tableless-header source table leaves an empty header row — suppress empty `<thead>` in all three builders; axis labels quoted in a caption must match the figure text exactly; a restating sentence ("That is, …") must not be dropped.
  期权战略投资抽检第50轮问题族：图注对“(!)”标记的解释方向写反（公式在上文却写“下文”）：不得臆测原书标记含义，须重算；原书无表头的表在 Markdown 里留空表头行，三个构建器均不再渲染空 thead；图注引号内轴标签须与图内文字一致；“That is, …”复述句不得漏译。
- Options book spot-check round 51 family (P1): OCR misread ¼/¾ in the English source, the translator wrote the wrong number and a note claiming "the original prints it wrong, kept as printed". Audit result: of 96 translator notes that claim a printed-original error, 3 were OCR misreadings (029_ch25 ¼ vs ½, 034_ch29 50¾ vs 50¼, 041_ch35 "175" vs "17.5") — render the original page at high zoom before writing any "原书有误" note, and delete a note together with fixing the body number (renumber later notes in the chapter).
  期权战略投资抽检第51轮问题族（P1）：OCR 读错 ¼/¾，译者写出错误数字并加“原书印错、照录”的注。96条译注逐条回看原书页后，3条是 OCR 误读：写任何“原书有误”注之前，必须高倍渲染原书页；同时改正文数字并删注，顺延本章后续注号。
- Options book spot-check round 55 family: the "名称，即代码" apposition again (等效股票头寸，即ESP或等效期货头寸，即EFP) — write "名称（代码）" and keep locked-name placeholder counts unchanged.
  期权战略投资抽检第55轮问题族：“名称，即代码”插入语再次造成断句歧义：统一写“名称（代码）”，并保持锁定名占位符数量不变。
